Theorem 226.1 (Factorization Result)

Statement: The complex variety V defined by Factorization Result constraints admits a stratification into 90 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 757 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the Factorization Result condition on complex structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 904. The convergence follows from the Factorization Result estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 757. QED.
